HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_003028ad1d8a3599b86e","text":"92:19’s “no one has a favor with him” made the scatteredness social as well as moral: people are dispersed into creditor/debtor networks unless purified giving exits that network. 92:13 also made the divergence temporal, with first and last held together by God.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001488/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_00e32d2d853af9708a15","text":"1. **Warning as fear that awakens caution.** Evidence: `ن ذ ر` B001 (warning that awakens fear/caution), `ن و ر` B002 (burning fire), `ل ظ ي` B001 (pure blazing flame). Structural cue: warning follows guidance and ownership. Mechanism: the warning operationalizes guidance for those nearing harm. Abductive move: I infer that fear is medicinal when it produces وقاية. Reading change: the fire is not introduced as spectacle; it is an alarm designed to make caution possible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001357/B003","root_001564/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_04192ea0daaeb2136c17","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:14:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B007\"]],[\"92:14:3\",\"root_001357\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Öfke ve düşmanlığın alevlenmesi boyutları, sonraki inkârın içindeki karşıtlığın dışarıda ateş biçimi almasını düşündürür; bu psikolojik bağ spekülatiftir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0a538041dbba9ed8f908","text":"92:15 converted the visible flame into an actual encounter, confirming the warning-beacon mechanism. 92:17 added the less obvious counterpart: because the danger remains blazing while one agent is placed aside from it, warning can be read as enabling a boundary relation rather than promising removal of the danger itself.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_21e3bc1ee2d3a20654d1","text":"2. **Fire as illumination and boundary-marker.** Evidence: `ن و ر` B001 (light/illumination), B005 (beacon/landmark), activated by 92:2 daylight and 92:12 guidance. Structural cue: the same field can illuminate or burn. Mechanism: fire marks the dangerous edge of the wrong path. Abductive move: I infer a double function: punishment-fire also reveals route boundaries. Reading change: the blaze becomes a negative lighthouse.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001488/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_351928c76c60831e0ffc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:14:1\",\"root_001488\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Uyarı, korkuyu yalnız dehşet olarak bırakmaz; ileride ateşten uzak tutulmayla tamamlanan koruyucu sakınmayı uyandıran bir işlev kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001357/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_634782ad0b219e1f6e9f","text":"3. **Blazing as anger and social flare.** Evidence: `ل ظ ي` B003 (burning with anger), `ن و ر` B007 (enmity flaring), activated by 92:16 denial and turning away. Structural cue: the fire is attached to a social-moral type, not only a place. Mechanism: refused truth ignites hostility that later receives fiery form. Abductive move: I infer that eschatological flame externalizes an already-kindled antagonism. Reading change: تَلَظَّىٰ can expose the inner heat of denial.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001488/B001","root_001564/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7e4784f8c040b7707ebb","text":"1. **Fire as a visible warning-beacon.** `أَنذَرْتُكُمْ` anchors `ن ذ ر:B001` (warning that awakens fear and caution); `نَارًا` anchors `ن و ر:B002` (burning fire) while activating `ن و ر:B001` (illumination) and `ن و ر:B005` (beacon or landmark). Guidance in 92:12 and the immediately specified encounter with fire in 92:15 turn illumination into advance orientation: the danger can be seen before it is met. The packet gives warning, light branches, and order; the inferred arrow is that the fire’s visibility makes caution possible. The ayah therefore presents fire not only as threatened harm but also as the sign that exposes where a course leads.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001357/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8855a0fd42890b478005","text":"2. **A consequence already in active flare.** `تَلَظَّىٰ` anchors `ل ظ ي:B001` (pure flame flaring and blazing), while `ن و ر:B002` includes fire’s flickering movement. The verbal scene is followed by `يَصْلَىٰهَا` in 92:15 and by the behavioral description in 92:16, so warning, moving flame, encounter, and agent form a causal-looking chain. The chain’s direction from conduct to exposure is inferred; the packet directly supplies only sequence and relations. The fire reads as an active process toward which conduct is converging, not a static object placed at the end of the sentence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001488/B001","root_001564/B001","root_001564/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b3e8b0c0508436da0967","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:14:1\",\"root_001488\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:14:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateşin ışık ve işaret boyutu, uyarıyı yanlış yolun tehlikeli sınırını daha karşılaşmadan görünür kılan bir yol belirtecine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001357/B001","root_001564/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f526777383d91bfc7543","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:14:2\",\"root_001564\",[\"B002\"]],[\"92:14:3\",\"root_001357\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Alevin hareketli parlayışı, ateşi uzakta duran bir nesne değil, davranış yolunun kendisine doğru yaklaştığı etkin bir sonuç olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
